<!-- TWEAKS -->
<div id="tweaks-panel">
  <div class="tw-title">Tweaks</div>
  <div class="tw-group">
    <div class="tw-label">CTA principal</div>
    <div class="tw-opts">
      <button class="tw-btn active" onclick="setCta(0,this)">Réserver mon appel offert</button>
      <button class="tw-btn" onclick="setCta(1,this)">Je veux plus de clients</button>
      <button class="tw-btn" onclick="setCta(2,this)">Réserver 30 minutes offertes</button>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

<script>
const TWEAK_DEFAULTS=/*EDITMODE-BEGIN*/{"cta":0}/*EDITMODE-END*/;

/* CAROUSEL */
(function(){
  const track=document.getElementById('carousel-track');
  if(!track) return;
  const cards=track.querySelectorAll('.carousel-card');
  const dotsContainer=document.getElementById('carousel-dots');
  const CARD_W=260+20; // card width + gap
  let current=0;
  const total=cards.length;

  // Build dots
  cards.forEach((_,i)=>{
    const d=document.createElement('button');
    d.className='carousel-dot'+(i===0?' active':'');
    d.setAttribute('aria-label','Slide '+(i+1));
    d.onclick=()=>goTo(i);
    dotsContainer.appendChild(d);
  });

  function goTo(i){
    current=Math.max(0,Math.min(i,total-1));
    const padding=Math.max(40,(window.innerWidth-1200)/2+40);
    track.scrollTo({left:current*CARD_W,behavior:'smooth'});
    document.querySelectorAll('.carousel-dot').forEach((d,idx)=>d.classList.toggle('active',idx===current));
    document.getElementById('carousel-prev').disabled=current===0;
    document.getElementById('carousel-next').disabled=current===total-1;
  }

  window.carouselMove=function(dir){goTo(current+dir)};

  // Drag to scroll
  let isDown=false,startX,scrollLeft;
  track.addEventListener('mousedown',e=>{isDown=true;track.classList.add('dragging');startX=e.pageX-track.offsetLeft;scrollLeft=track.scrollLeft});
  track.addEventListener('mouseleave',()=>{isDown=false;track.classList.remove('dragging')});
  track.addEventListener('mouseup',()=>{isDown=false;track.classList.remove('dragging')});
  track.addEventListener('mousemove',e=>{if(!isDown)return;e.preventDefault();const x=e.pageX-track.offsetLeft;track.scrollLeft=scrollLeft-(x-startX)*1.5});

  // Sync dots on scroll
  track.addEventListener('scroll',()=>{
    const i=Math.round(track.scrollLeft/CARD_W);
    if(i!==current){current=i;document.querySelectorAll('.carousel-dot').forEach((d,idx)=>d.classList.toggle('active',idx===i));}
  },{passive:true});

  goTo(0);
})();

/* VIDEO CONTROLS */
const PAUSE_ICON='<line x1="6" y1="4" x2="6" y2="20"/><line x1="18" y1="4" x2="18" y2="20"/>';
const PLAY_ICON='<polygon points="5,3 19,12 5,21"/>';
const MUTE_ICON='<polygon points="11,5 6,9 2,9 2,15 6,15 11,19"/><line x1="23" y1="9" x2="17" y2="15"/><line x1="17" y1="9" x2="23" y2="15"/>';
const VOL_ICON='<polygon points="11,5 6,9 2,9 2,15 6,15 11,19"/><path d="M19.07 4.93a10 10 0 0 1 0 14.14"/><path d="M15.54 8.46a5 5 0 0 1 0 7.07"/>';

function vcPlay(n){
  const v=document.getElementById('vid-'+n);
  const play=document.getElementById('vplay-'+n);
  const wrap=document.getElementById('vwrap-'+n);
  const icon=document.getElementById('vicon-'+n);
  if(v.paused){
    // Pause all others
    [1,2,3,4,5].forEach(i=>{
      if(i!==n){
        const ov=document.getElementById('vid-'+i);
        if(ov&&!ov.paused){
          ov.pause();
          document.getElementById('vplay-'+i).classList.remove('hidden');
          document.getElementById('vwrap-'+i).classList.remove('playing');
          document.getElementById('vicon-'+i).innerHTML=PLAY_ICON;
        }
      }
    });
    v.muted=false;
    v.play();
    play.classList.add('hidden');
    wrap.classList.add('playing');
    icon.innerHTML=PAUSE_ICON;
  } else {
    v.pause();
    play.classList.remove('hidden');
    wrap.classList.remove('playing');
    icon.innerHTML=PLAY_ICON;
  }
}

function vcMute(n){
  const v=document.getElementById('vid-'+n);
  const icon=document.getElementById('vmute-'+n);
  const slider=document.getElementById('vvol-'+n);
  v.muted=!v.muted;
  icon.innerHTML=v.muted?MUTE_ICON:VOL_ICON;
  if(v.muted) slider.value=0; else slider.value=v.volume||1;
}

function vcVolume(e,n){
  const v=document.getElementById('vid-'+n);
  const icon=document.getElementById('vmute-'+n);
  v.volume=e.target.value;
  v.muted=e.target.value==0;
  icon.innerHTML=v.muted?MUTE_ICON:VOL_ICON;
}

function vcSeek(e,n){
  const v=document.getElementById('vid-'+n);
  const bar=e.currentTarget;
  const ratio=e.offsetX/bar.offsetWidth;
  v.currentTime=ratio*v.duration;
}

// Progress bar update
[1,2,3,4,5].forEach(n=>{
  const v=document.getElementById('vid-'+n);
  if(!v) return;
  v.addEventListener('timeupdate',()=>{
    const p=document.getElementById('vprog-'+n);
    if(p&&v.duration) p.style.width=(v.currentTime/v.duration*100)+'%';
  });
  v.addEventListener('ended',()=>{
    document.getElementById('vplay-'+n).classList.remove('hidden');
    document.getElementById('vwrap-'+n).classList.remove('playing');
    document.getElementById('vicon-'+n).innerHTML=PLAY_ICON;
    const p=document.getElementById('vprog-'+n);
    if(p) p.style.width='0%';
  });
});

/* HAMBURGER NAV */
function toggleNav(){
  const links=document.getElementById('nav-links');
  const btn=document.getElementById('nav-hamburger');
  const open=links.classList.toggle('open');
  btn.classList.toggle('open',open);
  btn.setAttribute('aria-expanded',open);
  document.body.style.overflow=open?'hidden':'';
}
function closeNav(){
  const links=document.getElementById('nav-links');
  const btn=document.getElementById('nav-hamburger');
  links.classList.remove('open');
  btn.classList.remove('open');
  btn.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');
  document.body.style.overflow='';
}
// Hide hamburger on desktop
const mq=window.matchMedia('(min-width:901px)');
function handleMQ(e){
  const hb=document.getElementById('nav-hamburger');
  if(hb) hb.style.display=e.matches?'none':'flex';
  if(e.matches) closeNav();
}
mq.addEventListener('change',handleMQ);
handleMQ(mq);

/* NAV SCROLL */
window.addEventListener('scroll',()=>{
  document.getElementById('navbar').classList.toggle('scrolled',window.scrollY>40);
},{passive:true});

/* HERO SECTOR ROTATION */
const sEl=document.getElementById('sector-display');
const sectors=["Enfin à la hauteur de votre expertise.","Qui génère des opportunités.","Reconnue dans votre secteur.","Qui travaille pour vous."];
let sIdx=0;
sEl.style.transition='opacity .35s ease,transform .35s ease';
function animateSector(){
  sEl.style.opacity='0';sEl.style.transform='translateY(10px)';
  setTimeout(()=>{sIdx=(sIdx+1)%sectors.length;sEl.textContent=sectors[sIdx];sEl.style.opacity='1';sEl.style.transform='none'},300);
}
setInterval(animateSector,3200);

/* 4D SCROLL */
const arcIds=['arc1','arc2','arc3','arc4'],nodeIds=['node1','node2','node3','node4'],nlIds=['nl1','nl2','nl3','nl4'],panelIds=['panel1','panel2','panel3','panel4'];
let currentD4Step=0;
function setD4Step(n){
  if(n===currentD4Step)return;currentD4Step=n;
  panelIds.forEach((id,i)=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.classList.toggle('active',i===n)});
  arcIds.forEach((id,i)=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.classList.toggle('drawn',i<=n)});
  nodeIds.forEach((id,i)=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.classList.toggle('active',i<=n)});
  nlIds.forEach((id,i)=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.classList.toggle('active',i<=n)});
}
function updateD4Scroll(){
  const c=document.getElementById('d4-scroll-container');if(!c)return;
  const rect=c.getBoundingClientRect(),scrollable=c.offsetHeight-window.innerHeight;
  if(scrollable<=0)return;
  setD4Step(Math.min(3,Math.floor(Math.max(0,Math.min(1,-rect.top/scrollable))*4)));
}
window.addEventListener('scroll',updateD4Scroll,{passive:true});
setTimeout(()=>setD4Step(0),400);

/* REVEAL */
/* REVEAL — safe animation */
// Add pre-anim only after page load to avoid flash
window.addEventListener('load',()=>{
  document.querySelectorAll('.reveal').forEach(el=>{
    el.classList.add('pre-anim');
  });
  const obs=new IntersectionObserver(entries=>{
    entries.forEach(e=>{
      if(e.isIntersecting){
        e.target.classList.remove('pre-anim');
        e.target.classList.add('visible');
        obs.unobserve(e.target);
      }
    });
  },{threshold:0.08,rootMargin:'0px 0px -20px 0px'});
  document.querySelectorAll('.reveal').forEach(el=>obs.observe(el));
});

/* FAQ */
function toggleFaq(btn){
  const a=btn.nextElementSibling,open=btn.classList.contains('open');
  document.querySelectorAll('.faq-q').forEach(q=>{q.classList.remove('open');q.nextElementSibling.classList.remove('open')});
  if(!open){btn.classList.add('open');a.classList.add('open');}
}

/* RS CHIP TOGGLE */
document.querySelectorAll('.rs-chip').forEach(chip=>{
  const cb=chip.querySelector('input[type="checkbox"]');
  chip.addEventListener('click',function(e){
    e.preventDefault();
    cb.checked=!cb.checked;
    chip.classList.toggle('checked',cb.checked);
  });
});

/* AUTRE SECTEUR */
function toggleAutreSecteur(sel){
  document.getElementById('autre-secteur-wrap').style.display=sel.value==='autre'?'block':'none';
}

/* FORM — redirect to Crococlick */
async function handleSubmit(e){
  e.preventDefault();
  const btn=document.getElementById('form-submit-btn');
  btn.textContent='Un instant…';btn.disabled=true;
  // Send to Formspree — all form fields
  fetch('https://formspree.io/f/xojrbvqy',{
    method:'POST',body:new FormData(e.target),headers:{'Accept':'application/json'}
  }).catch(()=>{});
  // Redirect to Crococlick calendar
  setTimeout(()=>{
    window.location.href='https://link.crococlick.com/widget/bookings/joseph-khan';
  },400);
}

/* CTAs */
const ctas=["Réserver mon appel offert","Je veux plus de clients","Réserver 30 minutes offertes"];
const ctaIds=['hero-cta-btn','final-cta-btn','form-submit-btn','compare-cta-btn'];
function setCta(i,btn){
  ctaIds.forEach(id=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.textContent=ctas[i]});
  btn.parentElement.querySelectorAll('.tw-btn').forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('active'));
  btn.classList.add('active');
  window.parent.postMessage({type:'__edit_mode_set_keys',edits:{cta:i}},'*');
}
(function(){const t=ctas[TWEAK_DEFAULTS.cta||0];ctaIds.forEach(id=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.textContent=t})})();

/* TWEAKS PANEL */
window.addEventListener('message',e=>{
  if(e.data?.type==='__activate_edit_mode')document.getElementById('tweaks-panel').classList.add('show');
  if(e.data?.type==='__deactivate_edit_mode')document.getElementById('tweaks-panel').classList.remove('show');
});
window.parent.postMessage({type:'__edit_mode_available'},'*');
</script>

<!-- ═══════════════════ COOKIE BANNER ═══════════════════ -->
<div id="cookieBanner" role="dialog" aria-label="Gestion des cookies" style="display:none">
  <div class="ck-bg"></div>
  <div class="ck-card">
    <div class="ck-icon">🍪</div>
    <div class="ck-content">
      <div class="ck-title">Nous utilisons des cookies</div>
      <div class="ck-text">
        Vision House utilise des cookies pour améliorer votre expérience, analyser le trafic et personnaliser le contenu.
        Vous pouvez accepter tous les cookies ou choisir vos préférences.
        <a class="ck-link" href="https://visionhouse.be/politique-cookies.html">En savoir plus</a>
      </div>
      <div class="ck-btns">
        <button class="ck-btn ck-decline" onclick="cookieChoice('decline')">Refuser</button>
        <button class="ck-btn ck-prefs" onclick="cookieTogglePrefs()">Personnaliser</button>
        <button class="ck-btn ck-accept" onclick="cookieChoice('accept')">Tout accepter</button>
      </div>
    </div>
    <button class="ck-close" onclick="cookieChoice('decline')" aria-label="Fermer">×</button>
  </div>

  <!-- Preferences panel -->
  <div class="ck-prefs-panel" id="ckPrefsPanel">
    <div class="ck-prefs-title">Vos préférences</div>
    <div class="ck-toggle-row">
      <div class="ck-toggle-info">
        <span class="ck-toggle-name">Cookies essentiels</span>
        <span class="ck-toggle-desc">Nécessaires au fonctionnement du site. Toujours actifs.</span>
      </div>
      <div class="ck-toggle ck-toggle-locked">
        <div class="ck-toggle-thumb"></div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="ck-toggle-row">
      <div class="ck-toggle-info">
        <span class="ck-toggle-name">Cookies analytiques</span>
        <span class="ck-toggle-desc">Nous aident à comprendre comment vous naviguez sur le site.</span>
      </div>
      <div class="ck-toggle" id="ckAnalytics" onclick="cookieToggle(this)" role="switch" aria-checked="false">
        <div class="ck-toggle-thumb"></div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="ck-toggle-row">
      <div class="ck-toggle-info">
        <span class="ck-toggle-name">Cookies marketing</span>
        <span class="ck-toggle-desc">Permettent d'afficher des publicités pertinentes pour vous.</span>
      </div>
      <div class="ck-toggle" id="ckMarketing" onclick="cookieToggle(this)" role="switch" aria-checked="false">
        <div class="ck-toggle-thumb"></div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <button class="ck-btn ck-accept" style="width:100%;margin-top:.8rem" onclick="cookieSavePrefs()">Enregistrer mes choix</button>
  </div>
</div>

<style>
/* ── Cookie Banner — Glass ── */
#cookieBanner{position:fixed;bottom:0;left:0;right:0;z-index:9000;padding:1rem clamp(.75rem,3vw,1.5rem);animation:ckSlideUp .5s cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1) both}
@keyframes ckSlideUp{from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(24px)}to{opacity:1;transform:translateY(0)}}
#cookieBanner.ck-hide{animation:ckSlideDown .4s cubic-bezier(.4,0,1,1) both}
@keyframes ckSlideDown{from{opacity:1;transform:translateY(0)}to{opacity:0;transform:translateY(24px)}}

.ck-bg{position:absolute;inset:0;pointer-events:none}

.ck-card{
  position:relative;
  max-width:780px;margin:0 auto;
  display:flex;align-items:flex-start;gap:16px;
  padding:1.1rem 1.4rem 1.1rem 1.2rem;
  border-radius:20px;
  background:rgba(255,255,255,.72);
  backdrop-filter:blur(24px) saturate(180%);
  -webkit-backdrop-filter:blur(24px) saturate(180%);
  border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.55);
  box-shadow:0 8px 40px rgba(12,3,64,.14),0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,.8) inset,0 -1px 0 rgba(12,3,64,.06) inset;
}

.ck-icon{font-size:1.4rem;line-height:1;flex-shrink:0;margin-top:2px;filter:drop-shadow(0 2px 4px rgba(0,0,0,.15))}

.ck-content{flex:1;min-width:0}
.ck-title{font-family:'Montserrat',sans-serif;font-size:.9rem;font-weight:700;color:#0c0340;margin-bottom:.35rem;letter-spacing:-.01em}
.ck-text{font-size:.8rem;color:rgba(12,3,64,.6);line-height:1.55;margin-bottom:.85rem}
.ck-link{color:#092969;font-weight:600;text-decoration:underline;text-underline-offset:2px;transition:color .15s}
.ck-link:hover{color:#0c0340}

.ck-btns{display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center}

.ck-btn{
  padding:8px 18px;border-radius:100px;
  font-family:'Lato',sans-serif;font-size:.8rem;font-weight:700;
  cursor:pointer;border:none;transition:all .18s;
  white-space:nowrap;
}
.ck-decline{
  background:rgba(12,3,64,.06);
  color:rgba(12,3,64,.55);
  border:1px solid rgba(12,3,64,.1);
}
.ck-decline:hover{background:rgba(12,3,64,.1);color:#0c0340}
.ck-prefs{
  background:rgba(9,41,105,.07);
  color:#092969;
  border:1px solid rgba(9,41,105,.15);
}
.ck-prefs:hover{background:rgba(9,41,105,.13);color:#0c0340}
.ck-accept{
  background:#0c0340;color:#fff;
  box-shadow:0 4px 14px rgba(12,3,64,.3);
}
.ck-accept:hover{background:#092969;transform:translateY(-1px);box-shadow:0 6px 18px rgba(12,3,64,.35)}

.ck-close{
  position:absolute;top:.7rem;right:.8rem;
  width:26px;height:26px;border-radius:50%;
  background:rgba(12,3,64,.07);border:none;
  color:rgba(12,3,64,.45);font-size:16px;
  cursor:pointer;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;
  transition:background .15s,color .15s;line-height:1;
  flex-shrink:0;
}
.ck-close:hover{background:rgba(12,3,64,.14);color:#0c0340}

/* Prefs panel */
.ck-prefs-panel{
  display:none;
  max-width:780px;margin:.5rem auto 0;
  padding:1rem 1.2rem;
  border-radius:16px;
  background:rgba(255,255,255,.68);
  backdrop-filter:blur(20px) saturate(160%);
  -webkit-backdrop-filter:blur(20px) saturate(160%);
  border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.5);
  box-shadow:0 4px 24px rgba(12,3,64,.1),0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,.8) inset;
}
.ck-prefs-panel.show{display:block;animation:ckFadeIn .25s ease}
@keyframes ckFadeIn{from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(-6px)}to{opacity:1;transform:translateY(0)}}
.ck-prefs-title{font-family:'Montserrat',sans-serif;font-size:.82rem;font-weight:700;color:#0c0340;margin-bottom:.75rem;letter-spacing:.04em;text-transform:uppercase}

.ck-toggle-row{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:12px;padding:.55rem 0;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(12,3,64,.06)}
.ck-toggle-row:last-of-type{border-bottom:none}
.ck-toggle-info{flex:1;min-width:0}
.ck-toggle-name{font-size:.82rem;font-weight:700;color:#0c0340;display:block;margin-bottom:2px}
.ck-toggle-desc{font-size:.74rem;color:rgba(12,3,64,.5);line-height:1.4}

/* Toggle switch */
.ck-toggle{
  width:40px;height:22px;border-radius:11px;
  background:rgba(12,3,64,.12);
  position:relative;cursor:pointer;flex-shrink:0;
  transition:background .22s;border:1px solid rgba(12,3,64,.1);
}
.ck-toggle[aria-checked="true"]{background:#0c0340;border-color:#0c0340}
.ck-toggle-thumb{
  position:absolute;top:2px;left:2px;
  width:16px;height:16px;border-radius:50%;
  background:#fff;
  box-shadow:0 1px 4px rgba(12,3,64,.25);
  transition:transform .22s cubic-bezier(.34,1.56,.64,1);
}
.ck-toggle[aria-checked="true"] .ck-toggle-thumb{transform:translateX(18px)}
.ck-toggle-locked{background:rgba(12,3,64,.2);cursor:default;border-color:rgba(12,3,64,.15)}
.ck-toggle-locked .ck-toggle-thumb{transform:translateX(18px);background:#fff}

@media(max-width:640px){
  .ck-card{flex-wrap:wrap;gap:10px;padding:1rem 1.1rem}
  .ck-icon{display:none}
  .ck-btns{gap:6px}
  .ck-btn{padding:7px 14px;font-size:.76rem}
  .ck-close{top:.6rem;right:.6rem}
}
</style>

<script>
(function(){
  // Show banner if no consent stored
  if(!localStorage.getItem('vh_cookie_consent')){
    var b=document.getElementById('cookieBanner');
    if(b)b.style.display='block';
  }
})();

function cookieChoice(choice){
  localStorage.setItem('vh_cookie_consent',choice);
  var b=document.getElementById('cookieBanner');
  if(!b)return;
  b.classList.add('ck-hide');
  setTimeout(function(){b.style.display='none';b.classList.remove('ck-hide');},420);
}

function cookieTogglePrefs(){
  var p=document.getElementById('ckPrefsPanel');
  if(!p)return;
  p.classList.toggle('show');
}

function cookieToggle(el){
  var checked=el.getAttribute('aria-checked')==='true';
  el.setAttribute('aria-checked',!checked);
}

function cookieSavePrefs(){
  var analytics=document.getElementById('ckAnalytics').getAttribute('aria-checked')==='true';
  var marketing=document.getElementById('ckMarketing').getAttribute('aria-checked')==='true';
  var prefs={analytics:analytics,marketing:marketing,essential:true};
  localStorage.setItem('vh_cookie_consent','custom');
  localStorage.setItem('vh_cookie_prefs',JSON.stringify(prefs));
  cookieChoice('custom');
}
</script>
